} // v5.2.0: Persist observed historical features. } function observedFeature(id,row,profile){ if(!profile)return null; const course=Number(row.course_used),c=(profile.courses||[]).find(x=>Number(x.course)===course); const records=profile.recent||[]; continue; } deferred++;reasons[category]=(reasons[category]||0)+1; const attempts=Number(row.attempts||0)+1; const minutes=reason==='official_race_cancelled'?1440:category==='snapshot_invalid'?60:category==='official_result_missing'?5:category==='result_incomplete_or_special'?10:Math.min(30,5*Math.pow(2,Math.min(attempts-1,3))); const next=new Date(now+minutes*60000).toISOString(); try{ await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO snapshot_check_retries (snapshot_id,attempts,last_reason,last_checked_at,next_retry_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(snapshot_id) DO UPDATE SET attempts=excluded.attempts,last_reason=excluded.last_reason,last_checked_at=excluded.last_checked_at,next_retry_at=excluded.next_retry_at`).bind(row.id,attempts,reason,new Date(now).toISOString(),next).run(); }catch(logError){console.log('retry-log write failed:',String(logError?.message||logError));} console.log(`auto-check: snapshot ${row.id} deferred (${category}): ${reason}`); } } console.log(`auto-check: pending=${rows.length} due=${due.length} attempted=${checked} settled=${settled} cancelled=${cancelled} deferred=${deferred} reasons=${JSON.stringify(reasons)}`); return {check_attempted:checked,check_settled:settled,check_deferred:deferred, check_reason:checked?(deferred?'some_deferred':cancelled?'verified_cancellations_excluded':'completed'):'no_due_results', error_message:deferred?'check deferred: '+JSON.stringify(reasons).slice(0,180):''}; } async function runScheduledAutoSnapshots(env){ if(!env.DB||!env.API_WRITE_KEY)return {save_reason:'missing_binding_or_key'}; const now=Date.now(),today=jstDate(),daily=await getDaily(today); if(daily.error)return {save_reason:'upstream_unavailable'}; const prior=await env.DB.prepare('SELECT venue_number,race_number FROM prediction_snapshots WHERE race_date=? } // Final-phase snapshots are separate model-version records; existing early snapshots remain immutable. // v5.5.51: bounded final-only monitor; preserve the existing 120-second validation cutoff. const FINAL_WINDOW_MS=30*60000, FINAL_CUTOFF_MS=120000, FINAL_MONITOR_CRON='* * * * *'; function exhibitionCount(item){return Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=>numeric(item?.preview?.racers?.[String(i+1)]?.exhibition_time)).filter(n=>n!==null&&n>=5&&n<=10).length;} function finalWindow(close,now=Date.now()){return Number.isFinite(close)&&close-now>FINAL_CUTOFF_MS&&close-now<=FINAL_WINDOW_MS;} // Only a complete, race-matched, fresh official time table may supplement a snapshot. // ST and entry course are never fabricated. } async function prepareFinalExhibition(env,item,date,venue,race){ const probeStarted61=Date.now();const before=exhibitionCount(item); if(before===6)return {item,official_status:'not_needed_complete_feed'}; } // v5.5.53: constrain minute-monitor reads to schedule keys near the cutoff. // CROSS JOIN fixes the small wanted key set as the outer loop; composite indexes/PKs // (including v5.5.52 migration) let SQLite seek matching records instead of scanning the day. function finalMonitorKeys(daily,day,now){ const keys=[]; function detailedScore(item,legacy,profiles={}){ const ranked=legacy.ranking||[];const rows=[]; const rawST=ranked.map(r=>({boat:r.boat,st:numeric(r.average_start_timing)})); const validST=rawST.filter(r=>r.st!==null&&r.st>0&&r.st<=.5); if(f!==null&&f>=1)add('flying','F保有',f>=2?4:1.5,'F'+f+'：一律の能力判定ではなく暫定リスク補正'); if(l!==null&&l>=1)add('late','L保有',Math.min(2,l),'L'+l); if(st!==null&&st>.21&&st<=.5)add('late_st','平均ST遅れ',Math.min(3,(st-.21)*30),'平均ST '+st); if(st!==null&&validST.length>=4){ const rivals=validST.filter(x=>x.boat!==r.boat).map(x=>x.st); const median=[...rivals].sort((a,b)=>a-b)[Math.floor(rivals.length/2)]; tactical=Math.max(-3,Math.min(3,(median-st)*35)); } const penalty=Math.min(15,penalties.reduce((n,x)=>n-x.points,0)); const score=round1(Math.max(0,Math.min(100,base-penalty+tactical))); rows.push({...r,score,base_score:round1(base),risk_deduction:round1(penalty),tactical_adjustment:round1(tactical), detailed_factors:factors,detailed_penalties:penalties,detailed_missing:missing, detailed_coverage:{observed:20-missing.length,total:20}, detailed_warning:'20 slots include neutral missing inputs. ST/exhibition penalties may correlate with base features; experimental weights, not calibrated probabilities.'}); } rows.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||a.boat-b.boat); return {ranking:rows,scoring_revision:'20-factor-100point-risk-v1',scoring_method:'20 slots totaling 100; unavailable slots neutral at half weight; capped risk deduction 15; tactical adjustment +/-3; not empirically calibrated', weights:Object.fromEntries(DETAIL_FACTORS.map(([id,,w])=>[id,w])),total_fields:120, scoring_details:{factors:DETAIL_FACTORS.map(([id,label,max])=>({id,label,max_points:max})), unsupported:['motor trend/power','turn exhibition','tilt','venue/weather suitability','first-turn tactical history','attack vulnerability','exhibition-to-race consistency','recent form'], risk_penalty_cap:15,tactical_adjustment_range:[-3,3],warning:'Unsupported factors remain neutral. Not a validated predictive improvement.'}}; } const SHADOW_FEATURES=['national_win_rate','local_win_rate','motor_top_2_percent','average_start_timing','exhibition_time','exhibition_start_timing','course_advantage','rank_number']; function shadowModel(item,legacy,odds=null,profiles={}){ const detailed=detailedScore(item,legacy,profiles); const rows=detailed.ranking.map(r=>{ const breakdown=r.score_breakdown||{}; const score=r.score; const missing=SHADOW_FEATURES.filter(k=>breakdown[k]?.data_available===false); const boat=Number(r.boat); const raw=item.racers?.[String(boat)]||{}; const preview=item.preview?.racers?.[String(boat)]||{}; const actualCourse=numeric(preview.course_number); const course=Number.isInteger(actualCourse)&&actualCourse>=1&&actualCourse<=6?actualCourse:boat; const avgST=numeric(raw.average_start_timing); const exhibitST=numeric(preview.start_timing); // These are explicit heuristic features, not empirical win probabilities. const stQuality=avgST!==null&&avgST>0&&avgST<=.5?Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,(.18-avgST)/.12)):0; const exhibitQuality=exhibitST!==null&&exhibitST>=0&&exhibitST<=.5?Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,(.18-exhibitST)/.18)):0; const coursePrior=[0,1,.68,.49,.35,.23,.15][course]; const latent=(score-50)/18 + .35*stQuality + .12*exhibitQuality + .5*(coursePrior-.5); return {boat,score:Math.round(score*10)/10,latent,course,missing_fields:r.detailed_missing,missing_count:r.detailed_missing.length,base_score:r.base_score,risk_deduction:r.risk_deduction,tactical_adjustment:r.tactical_adjustment,detailed_factors:r.detailed_factors,detailed_penalties:r.detailed_penalties,detailed_coverage:r.detailed_coverage, start_quality:Math.round(stQuality*1000)/1000,exhibition_start_quality:Math.round(exhibitQuality*1000)/1000}; }); // Plackett-Luce ordering model: P(first,second,third) = sequential normalized weights. const weights=new Map(rows.map(r=>[r.boat,Math.exp(Math.max(-8,Math.min(8,r.latent)))])); const sum=rows.reduce((n,r)=>n+weights.get(r.boat),0); const combos=[]; for(const a of rows)for(const b of rows)for(const c of rows){ if(a.boat===b.boat||a.boat===c.boat||b.boat===c.boat)continue; const wa=weights.get(a.boat),wb=weights.get(b.boat),wc=weights.get(c.boat); const probability=(wa/sum)*(wb/(sum-wa))*(wc/(sum-wa-wb)); const combination=`${a.boat}-${b.boat}-${c.boat}`; const decimalOdds=odds&&Number(odds[combination]); const validOdds=Number.isFinite(decimalOdds)&&decimalOdds>=1&&decimalOdds<=100000; combos.push({combination,probability,odds:validOdds?decimalOdds:null, expected_return_ratio:validOdds?probability*decimalOdds:null}); } const total=combos.reduce((n,c)=>n+c.probability,0); for(const c of combos)c.probability/=total; const byProb=[...combos].sort((a,b)=>b.probability-a.probability||a.combination.localeCompare(b.combination)); const oddsAvailable=combos.every(c=>c.odds!==null); const byEV=oddsAvailable?[...combos].sort((a,b)=>b.expected_return_ratio-a.expected_return_ratio||b.probability-a.probability):[]; const byBalanced=oddsAvailable?[...combos].sort((a,b)=>{ const sa=Math.log(Math.max(a.probability,1e-12))+.35*Math.log(Math.max(a.expected_return_ratio,1e-12)); const sb=Math.log(Math.max(b.probability,1e-12))+.35*Math.log(Math.max(b.expected_return_ratio,1e-12)); return sb-sa||a.combination.localeCompare(b.combination); }):[]; const six=arr=>arr.slice(0,6).map(x=>({combination:x.combination,stake_yen:100,estimated_probability:x.probability, odds:x.odds,expected_return_ratio:x.expected_return_ratio})); const missingCount=rows.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.missing_count,0); const confidence=missingCount>60?'low':missingCount>24?'limited':'unvalidated'; return {model:SHADOW_MODEL,status:'experimental_not_calibrated',date:legacy.date,venue:legacy.venue,race:legacy.race, phase:legacy.phase,source:legacy.source,closing_at:legacy.closing_at, no_post_race_inputs_verified:false,probability_sum:combos.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.probability,0), feature_coverage:{observed:120-missingCount,total:120,missing:missingCount},confidence, features:rows,probabilities:combos.sort((a,b)=>b.probability-a.probability), strategies:{legacy:six(legacy.tickets.map(t=>({combination:t.combination,probability:combos.find(c=>c.combination===t.combination)?.probability||0,odds:odds?.[t.combination]??null,expected_return_ratio:null}))), hit_rate:six(byProb),expected_value:oddsAvailable?six(byEV):null,balance:oddsAvailable?six(byBalanced):null}, odds_status:oddsAvailable?'complete':'unavailable_or_partial', recommended_live_strategy:'legacy_unchanged', scoring_details:detailed.scoring_details,notes:['Heuristic ordering probabilities only; NOT calibrated winning probabilities.','Missing features do not invent course-specific or weather measurements.', 'EV and balance strategies require a complete, time-stamped pre-race odds snapshot.','No wagers are placed.']}; } // Third-candidate experimental model. Separate from legacy scoring and live six-ticket policy. const THIRD_MODEL='relative-start-exhibition-development-v1'; function thirdCandidate(item,legacy){ const racers=legacy.ranking||[]; if(racers.length!==6)return null; const ordered=[...racers].sort((a,b)=>a.course_used-b.course_used); const validST=x=>Number.isFinite(x)&&x>0&&x<=0.5; const validEx=x=>Number.isFinite(x)&&x>=5&&x<=10; const details=ordered.map(r=>{ const inside=ordered.filter(x=>x.course_usedvalidST(x.average_start_timing)); const interaction=stGap===null||exGap===null?0: Math.max(-3,Math.min(3,(stGap*35)+(exGap*12))); const inner=ordered[0]; const isInner=r.boat===inner.boat; const insideAttack=inside.reduce((s,x)=>s+(validST(x.average_start_timing)&&st!==null?Math.max(0,(x.average_start_timing-st)*25):0),0); const escape=isInner?Math.max(-5,Math.min(5,3-insideAttack)):0; const attack=isInner?0:startEdge+exhibitionEdge+interaction; const headScore=r.score+attack+escape; // Late finishing order is treated separately: outside boats can benefit from inside disruption. const benefit=isInner?0:Math.max(0,Math.min(4,insideAttack*0.5)); missing/ambiguous HTML is never accepted. function parseP4OfficialTechnique(html,expected){ const tables=[...String(html).matchAll(/]*>[\s\S]*?<\/table>/gi)].map(x=>x[0]); const labels=[],orders=[];let techniqueTables=0,finishTables=0; } function compareTechnique67(x,row){ if(x.status!=='verified_table')return 'not_checked'; if(!row.feed_source)return 'no_existing_evidence'; if(row.feed_winning!==x.winning||Number(row.feed_boat)!==x.boat||Number(row.feed_id)!==x.id)return 'identity_conflict'; }); // Scenario mixture: independently rank all 120 trifectas under alternative race developments. // All scenario weights and modifiers are provisional heuristics, not fitted probabilities. // Each scenario normalizes to one before mixing; no artificial ticket divergence. const byBoat=new Map(rows.map(x=>[x.boat,x])); const inputByBoat=new Map(inputs.map(x=>[x.boat,x])); const scenarios=[ {id:'inside_hold',label:'イン逃げ・内残り',weight:.44}, {id:'inside_sashi',label:'内差し',weight:.22}, {id:'center_attack',label:'センターまくり',weight:.19}, {id:'outside_break',label:'まくり差し・外伸び',weight:.15} ]; const startEdge=x=>{ const inp=inputByBoat.get(x.boat); const st=inp?.exst??inp?.st; return st===null||st===undefined?0:Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,(.18-st)/.15)); }; const tactic=x=>{ const n=x.features.course_tactics; return n===null?0:(n-.5)*2; }; const score=(x,scenario,position)=>{ const lane=x.lane,fast=startEdge(x),tech=tactic(x); let boost=0; if(scenario==='inside_hold')boost=(lane===1?1.0:lane===2?.22:-.13*(lane-2))+(position===1?.35*fast:.12*fast); if(scenario==='inside_sashi')boost=(lane===2?.95:lane===1?.15:lane===3?.12:-.12)+(position===1?.28*fast:.08*fast)+.12*tech; if(scenario==='center_attack')boost=((lane===3||lane===4)?.9:lane===1?-.55:0)+(position===1?.55*fast:.18*fast)+.2*tech; if(scenario==='outside_break')boost=(lane>=4?.68:lane===3?.3:lane===1?-.38:0)+(position===1?.4*fast:.12*fast)+.25*tech; // A modest scenario correction relative to the existing historical-form strength. return Math.exp(Math.max(-8,Math.min(8,x.latent+.62*boost))); }; const combos=[]; for(const a of rows)for(const b of rows)for(const c of rows){ if(a.boat===b.boat||a.boat===c.boat||b.boat===c.boat)continue; combos.push({combination:`${a.boat}-${b.boat}-${c.boat}`,boats:[a.boat,b.boat,c.boat]}); } const scenarioProbabilities={}; for(const scenario of scenarios){ const scores=new Map(rows.map(x=>[x.boat,[1,2,3].map(pos=>score(x,scenario.id,pos))])); const raw=combos.map(({boats:[a,b,c]})=>{ const a1=scores.get(a)[0],b2=scores.get(b)[1],c3=scores.get(c)[2]; const sum1=rows.reduce((n,x)=>n+scores.get(x.boat)[0],0); const sum2=rows.filter(x=>x.boat!==a).reduce((n,x)=>n+scores.get(x.boat)[1],0); const sum3=rows.filter(x=>x.boat!==a&&x.boat!==b).reduce((n,x)=>n+scores.get(x.boat)[2],0); return (a1/sum1)*(b2/sum2)*(c3/sum3); }); const totalRaw=raw.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0); scenarioProbabilities[scenario.id]=raw.map(v=>v/totalRaw); } // Frozen pre-race decomposition. This is a heuristic distribution, not a calibrated probability. // Persist per-scenario 120-way predictions before any result is available. const scenarioDistributions=scenarios.map(sc=>({ id:sc.id,label:sc.label,heuristic_weight:sc.weight, trifecta_probabilities:combos.map((combo,i)=>({ combination:combo.combination, heuristic_probability:Number(scenarioProbabilities[sc.id][i].toFixed(10)) })) })); const probabilities=combos.map((combo,i)=>({combination:combo.combination, probability:scenarios.reduce((sum,sc)=>sum+sc.weight*scenarioProbabilities[sc.id][i],0)})); probabilities.sort((a,b)=>b.probability-a.probability||a.combination.localeCompare(b.combination)); const sum=probabilities.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.probability,0); for(const x of probabilities)x.probability=Number((x.probability/sum).toFixed(10)); const six=probabilities.slice(0,6); const hitMass=six.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.probability,0); const coverage=rows.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.feature_count,0)/(rows.length*15); const tickets=six.map(x=>({combination:x.combination,stake_yen:100, heuristic_probability:x.probability,odds:null,expected_return_ratio:null})); return {model:FOURTH_MODEL,version:'v5-technique-evidence-provisional',date:legacy.date,venue:legacy.venue,race:legacy.race, phase:legacy.phase,closing_at:legacy.closing_at,created_at_utc:new Date().toISOString(), source_snapshot_model:legacy.model,...(item.final_exhibition_evidence?{final_exhibition_evidence:item.final_exhibition_evidence}:{}),method:'Historical-form strength + normalized four-scenario trifecta probability mixture', rows,scenarios:scenarios.map(x=>({id:x.id,label:x.label,heuristic_weight:x.weight})),scenario_distributions:scenarioDistributions,probabilities,tickets,total_stake_yen:600,top_six_probability_mass:Number(hitMass.toFixed(6)), feature_coverage_ratio:Number(coverage.toFixed(4)),odds_status:'not_recorded', expected_value:null,balance_score:null, selection:'research_only_no_verified_prerace_odds', calibration_status:'not_calibrated', notes:['All 120 combinations and scenario weights are heuristic, not calibrated probabilities.', 'Scenario modifiers use course, available start timing and historical tactics; no physical race simulation or weather claim.', 'Feature weights are provisional; no trained model is claimed.', 'Historical form uses strictly earlier D1 observations only; recent form uses up to 60 rows, venue form uses all available prior dates in D1.', 'Historical features reuse original normalization and retain sample counts; unknown winning techniques do not imply zero attacks.', 'Official exhibition time can fill missing final-phase times only; official exhibition ST/entry are not verified.', 'Meet ST is an exhibition-ST proxy; course tactics use prior-date winner identity matched evidence (unofficial feed or official result page); conflicts are excluded.', 'No EV, profitable bet or buy recommendation without verified timestamped pre-race odds.', 'Simulation only. No real purchases or changes to existing prediction strategies.']}; } async function ensureFourthTable(env){ await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS prediction_fourth_v1 ( snapshot_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,race_date TEXT NOT NULL,venue INTEGER NOT NULL,race INTEGER NOT NULL, phase TEXT NOT NULL,model_version TEXT NOT NULL,prediction_json TEXT NOT NULL, created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)`).run(); } async function saveFourthCandidate(env,snapshotId,item,legacy,shadow){ if(!env.DB||!Number.isSafeInteger(Number(snapshotId))||!shadow)return false; const close=jstMs(legacy.closing_at); if(!Number.isFinite(close)||Date.now()>=close)return false; const context=await fourthContext(env,item,legacy,shadow); if(Date.now()>=close)return false; const fourth=fourthCandidate(item,legacy,shadow,context); if(!fourth)return false; try{const q=quality580(fourth,item);if(q)fourth.quality_research580=q;}catch(e){console.log("quality research skipped");} await ensureFourthTable(env); const result=await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO prediction_fourth_v1 (snapshot_id,race_date,venue,race,phase,model_version,prediction_json) SELECT ?,?,?,?,?,?,? Fail closed if layout is unrecognized. // An official page fetch is NOT itself proof that parsing succeeded. function officialPayText(fragment){ return String(fragment||'').replace(/]*>[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi,' ') .replace(/]*>[\s\S]*?<\/style>/gi,' ') .replace(/<[^>]+>/g,' ').replace(/ | /gi,' ') .replace(/¥|¥/gi,'¥').replace(/&/gi,'&') .replace(/&#(\d+);/g,(_,n)=>String.fromCharCode(Number(n))) .replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim(); } function parseOfficialPayHTML(html,expectedVenues){ const out=new Map(),diag={table_count:0,parsed:0,rows_examined:0,rejected:0,layout_mismatch:0}; const tables=[...html.matchAll(/]*>[\s\S]*?<\/table>/gi)].map(x=>x[0]); // Venue ordering must match the official site's venue number ordering. // A group is accepted only when its 12 race rows have a consistent 3-column-per-venue layout. let groupIndex=0; for(const table of tables){ const rows=[...table.matchAll(/]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/tr>/gi)].map(x=>x[1]); const raceRows=[]; for(const row of rows){ const cells=[...row.matchAll(/]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/t[dh]>/gi)].map(x=>officialPayText(x[1])); if(cells.length<4)continue; const first=cells[0].match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*R$/i); if(first&&Number(first[1])>=1&&Number(first[1])<=12)raceRows.push({race:Number(first[1]),cells}); } if(raceRows.length!==12||new Set(raceRows.map(x=>x.race)).size!==12)continue; diag.table_count++; const width=raceRows[0].cells.length-1; if(width%3!==0||width<3||width>15||raceRows.some(x=>x.cells.length!==width+1)){ diag.layout_mismatch++;continue; } const count=width/3; const group=expectedVenues.slice(groupIndex,groupIndex+count); if(group.length!==count){diag.layout_mismatch++;continue;} groupIndex+=count; for(const row of raceRows){ diag.rows_examined++; for(let i=0;i]*>[\s\S]*?<\/table>/gi)].map(x=>x[0]); let groupIndex=0; for(const table of tables){ const rows=[...table.matchAll(/]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/tr>/gi)].map(x=>x[1]); const rr=[]; for(const row of rows){ const cells=[...row.matchAll(/]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/t[dh]>/gi)].map(x=>officialPayText(x[1])); const m=cells[0]?.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*R$/i); if(m&&Number(m[1])>=1&&Number(m[1])<=12)rr.push({race:Number(m[1]),cells}); } if(rr.length!==12||new Set(rr.map(x=>x.race)).size!==12)continue; const widths=rr.map(x=>x.cells.length-1); const validWidths=[...new Set(widths.filter(x=>x%3===0&&x>=3&&x<=15))]; let count=validWidths.length===1?validWidths[0]/3:null; if(count===null){ const header=officialPayText(table.slice(0,Math.min(table.length,3500))); const venueMatches=expectedVenues.filter(v=>header.includes(VENUES[v-1])); if(venueMatches.length===1&&venueMatches[0]===expectedVenues[groupIndex])count=1; } if(!count||groupIndex+count>expectedVenues.length){diagnostics.ambiguous_tables++;continue;} const group=expectedVenues.slice(groupIndex,groupIndex+count); groupIndex+=count; diagnostics.candidate_tables++; if(count>1)diagnostics.multi_venue_tables++; for(const row of rr){ // Only accept explicit race cancellation inside a positionally aligned // 3-cell venue group. Never infer cancellation from a missing payout. if(row.cells.length!==1+count*3)continue; for(let i=0;i2500000){info.status='oversized_html';return info;} const expected=[...new Set(races.map(r=>r.venue))].sort((a,b)=>a-b); const parsed=parseOfficialPayHTML(html,expected); const cancelledPay=parseOfficialCancelledPayRows(html,expected); info.cancelled_keys=[...cancelledPay.cancelled];info.cancellation_parse=cancelledPay.diagnostics; Object.assign(info,parsed.diagnostics); info.status=parsed.records.size?'parsed':'unrecognized_layout_or_no_payouts'; for(const r of races){ const official=parsed.records.get(r.venue+'-'+r.race); if(!official)continue; if(r.status==='settled'&&(r.winning!==official.winning||r.payout_per_100!==official.payout)){ info.conflicts++;r.result_conflict=true; r.official_conflict={winning:official.winning,payout_per_100:official.payout}; // Official payout has priority, but conflicts remain visible for audit. } if(r.status!=='settled')info.added++; r.status='settled';r.winning=official.winning;r.payout_per_100=official.payout; r.result_issue=null;r.result_origin='official_pay';r.officially_verified=true; } for(const r of races){ if(!cancelledPay.cancelled.has(r.venue+'-'+r.race)||r.officially_verified===true)continue; r.status='cancelled';r.winning=null;r.payout_per_100=null; r.officially_verified=false;r.cancellation_verified=true; r.cancellation_source='official_pay';r.cancellation_source_url=url; r.result_issue='official_race_cancelled'; } return info; }catch(e){info.status='fetch_failed';info.error=String(e?.message||e).slice(0,180);return info;} } // Official venue cancellation probe. Fail closed: absent/ambiguous notice is UNKNOWN, not cancelled. async function officialVenueCancellation(date,venue){ const url=`https://www.boatrace.jp/owpc/pc/race/racelist?hd=${date.replace(/-/g,'')}&jcd=${String(venue).padStart(2,'0')}&rno=1`; const result={status:'unknown',source_url:url,officially_verified:false}; try{ const response=await fetch(url,{headers:{Accept:'text/html'},redirect:'follow',cache:'no-store'}); if(!response.ok)return {...result,reason:'http_'+response.status}; if(!/html/i.test(response.headers.get('content-type')||''))return {...result,reason:'not_html'}; const html=await response.text();if(html.length>1500000)return {...result,reason:'oversized'}; // Restrict to page title/main content; a site-wide announcement is not enough. const main=html.match(/]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/main>/i)?.[1]||html.match(/]*class=["'][^"']*(?:contents|mainContents|main-content)[^"']*["'][^>]*>([\s\S]{0,150000}?)<\/div>/i)?.[1]||''; const text=officialPayText(main); if(!text)return {...result,reason:'main_not_identified'}; // Only an explicit whole-meeting cancellation notice qualifies. const notice=/(?:本日|当日|本開催|開催日|全レース|全競走)[^。\n]{0,35}(?:開催中止|全レース中止|全競走中止)|(?:開催中止|全レース中止|全競走中止)[^。\n]{0,25}(?:本日|当日|全レース|全競走)/.test(text); return notice?{status:'cancelled',source_url:url,officially_verified:true,scope:'venue_day',reason:'explicit_official_meeting_cancellation'}:{...result,reason:'no_explicit_meeting_cancellation'}; }catch(e){return {...result,reason:'fetch_failed'};} } async function getOfficialCancelledVenues(date,races){ // Do not probe venues with official settled results: their entire day was not cancelled. const venues=[...new Set(races.map(r=>r.venue))].filter(v=>!races.some(r=>r.venue===v&&r.officially_verified===true&&r.status==='settled')); const results=await Promise.all(venues.map(async v=>[v,await officialVenueCancellation(date,v)])); return {cancelled_venues:results.filter(([v,s])=>s.status==='cancelled').map(([v])=>v),checks:results.map(([venue,s])=>({venue,...s}))}; } // Durable result ledger: never downgrade a previously obtained valid trifecta to pending. async function mergeRaceResultLedger(env,date,races){ if(!env.DB)return {races,ledger_status:'db_unavailable',recovered:0}; ON CONFLICT(race_date,venue,race) DO UPDATE SET winning=excluded.winning,payout_per_100=excluded.payout_per_100,result_origin=excluded.result_origin,officially_verified=1 WHERE race_result_ledger.winning IS NOT excluded.winning OR race_result_ledger.payout_per_100 IS NOT excluded.payout_per_100 OR race_result_ledger.result_origin IS NOT excluded.result_origin OR race_result_ledger.officially_verified IS NOT 1` ).bind(date,r.venue,r.race,r.winning,r.payout_per_100,'official_pay',1))); } const stored=(newUnofficial.length||officialRows.length)?await env.DB.prepare('SELECT venue,race,winning,payout_per_100,result_origin,officially_verified FROM race_result_ledger WHERE race_date=?').bind(date).all():initial; }else if(r.status==='settled'&&!r.result_origin)r.result_origin='upstream'; } return {races,ledger_status:'ok',recovered}; }catch(e){return {races,ledger_status:'error',ledger_error:String(e?.message||e).slice(0,180),recovered:0};} } // v5.2.9: Durable diagnostics for scheduled analysis and cancellation classification. // Separate from prediction, result, ticket and verified cancellation tables. async function ensureAnalysisRunLog(env){ await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS automatic_analysis_run_logs_v529 ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, started_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL, finished_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, processed_json TEXT NOT NULL, error_message TEXT, cancellation_json TEXT NOT NULL )`).run(); } async function cancellationDiagnostic(env){ await ensureCancellationLog(env); const registered=await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM verified_venue_cancellations').first(); const retry=await env.DB.prepare(`SELECT last_reason,COUNT(*) AS n FROM snapshot_check_retries WHERE last_reason LIKE '%cancel%' GROUP BY last_reason ORDER BY n DESC LIMIT 12`).all(); return {verified_venue_days:Number(registered?.n||0),verified_race_cancellations_today:(await readRaceCancellation(env,jstDate())).size,cancellation_retry_reasons:retry.results||[], note:'Race-level cancellation does not prove venue-wide cancellation. Retry reasons are historical state, not current official verification.'}; } // v5.2.8: D1 daily diagnostic snapshots. Read-only against prediction/result ledgers. // No upstream scraping, no post-race re-prediction, no changes to tickets or models. async function ensureAutomaticAnalysis(env){ await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS automatic_analysis_daily_v528 ( race_date TEXT PRIMARY KEY, generated_at_utc TEXT NOT NULL, source_rows INTEGER NOT NULL, eligible_rows INTEGER NOT NULL, official_rows INTEGER NOT NULL, provisional_rows INTEGER NOT NULL, pending_rows INTEGER NOT NULL, excluded_rows INTEGER NOT NULL, partial INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, summary_json TEXT NOT NULL )`).run(); } function automaticBucket(){return {snapshots:0,unique_races:0,exact_hits:0,first_misses:0,second_misses:0,third_misses:0,stake_yen:0,returned_yen:0,winning_first_rank:{},by_venue:{},by_strategy:{}};} function automaticFormat(b){return {...b,hit_rate_percent:b.snapshots?Math.round(b.exact_hits/b.snapshots*10000)/100:null,roi_percent:b.stake_yen?Math.round(b.returned_yen/b.stake_yen*10000)/100:null,profit_yen:b.returned_yen-b.stake_yen};} async function buildAutomaticAnalysis(env,date){ await ensureAutomaticAnalysis(env); await ensureRaceResultLedger(env); await ensureCancellationLog(env); const cap=1000; const count=await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM prediction_snapshots WHERE race_date=? } function newCalibration59(){return {n:0,invalid:0,loss:0,brier:0,mass:0,hits:0,bins:Array.from({length:5},()=>({n:0,mass:0,hits:0}))};} function observeCalibration59(a,c,tickets,winning){ const rows=c?.probabilities; if(!Array.isArray(rows)||rows.length!==120||new Set(rows.map(x=>x?.combination)).size!==120||rows.some(x=>!ticketOK(x?.combination)||typeof x.probability!=='number'||!Number.isFinite(x.probability)||x.probability<0||x.probability>1)){a.invalid++;return;} const sum=rows.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.probability,0);if(Math.abs(sum-1)>1e-6){a.invalid++;return;} const by=new Map(rows.map(x=>[x.combination,x.probability/sum]));const p=by.get(winning);if(!Number.isFinite(p)){a.invalid++;return;} const mass=Math.min(1,tickets.reduce((s,t)=>s+by.get(t.combination),0)),hit=Number(tickets.some(t=>t.combination===winning)); } function finishCalibration59(a){return {samples:a.n,invalid_distributions:a.invalid,mean_log_loss:a.n?a.loss/a.n:null,mean_multiclass_brier:a.n?a.brier/a.n:null,mean_top_six_probability_percent:a.n?100*a.mass/a.n:null,observed_top_six_hit_percent:a.n?100*a.hits/a.n:null,bins:a.bins.map((b,i)=>({from_percent:i*20,to_percent:(i+1)*20,samples:b.n,mean_probability_percent:b.n?100*b.mass/b.n:null,observed_hit_percent:b.n?100*b.hits/b.n:null}))};} // v5.5.48: read-only same-race four-candidate comparison; no scoring or writes. // v5.6.1: descriptive winner-distribution audit on the existing verified cohort. // No recalculation, training, ticket changes, SQL, network or writes. function headAudit561New(){return {n:0,invalid:0,actual:Array(6).fill(0),mass:Array(6).fill(0),max:Array(6).fill(0),ties:0,first:Array(6).fill(0),present:Array(6).fill(0),only:Array(6).fill(0),confusion:Array.from({length:6},()=>Array(6).fill(0)),loss:0,brier:0,bins:Array.from({length:10},()=>({n:0,p:0,y:0})),scenario:{},scenario_valid:0};} function headAudit561Distribution(rows,key){if(!Array.isArray(rows)||rows.length!==120||new Set(rows.map(r=>r?.combination)).size!==120||rows.some(r=>!ticketOK(r?.combination)||!Number.isFinite(r[key])||r[key]<0||r[key]>1))return null;const sum=rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+r[key],0);if(Math.abs(sum-1)>1e-6)return null;const p=Array(6).fill(0);for(const r of rows)p[Number(r.combination[0])-1]+=r[key]/sum;return p;} function headAudit561Observe(a,c,row){const p=headAudit561Distribution(c?.probabilities,'probability');if(!p||typeof row.fourth_model!=='string'||row.fourth_model!==c.model||!ticketOK(row.winning)||!Array.isArray(c.tickets)||c.tickets.length!==6||c.tickets.some(t=>!ticketOK(t?.combination))||new Set(c.tickets.map(t=>t.combination)).size!==6){a.invalid++;return;} const actual=Number(row.winning[0])-1,top=Math.max(...p),maxima=p.map((v,i)=>Math.abs(v-top)<1e-10?i:-1).filter(i=>i>=0),heads=new Set(c.tickets.map(t=>Number(t.combination[0])-1));a.n++;a.actual[actual]++;for(let i=0;i<6;i++)a.mass[i]+=p[i]; no model refit or new predictions. const LATENT_AUDIT_WEIGHTS562=Object.freeze({national:1.1,local:.35,motor:.45,start:.7,exhibition:.55,exhibition_start:.2,course:1.45,course_win:.45,course_place:.3,st_consistency:.2,meet_st_proxy:.18,course_tactics:.18,recent30_form:.45,recent60_form:.20,venue_form:.18}); function audit562Bucket(){return {n:0,p:0,y:0,brier:0,loss:0};} function audit562ObserveBucket(a,p,y){a.n++;a.p+=p;a.y+=y;a.brier+=(p-y)**2;a.loss-=Math.log(Math.max(y?p:1-p,1e-12));} function audit562FinishBucket(a){return {samples:a.n,mean_probability:a.n?a.p/a.n:null,observed_rate:a.n?a.y/a.n:null,gap:a.n?(a.p-a.y)/a.n:null,binary_brier:a.n?a.brier/a.n:null,binary_log_loss:a.n?a.loss/a.n:null};} function audit562New(){return {n:0,feature_invalid:0,latent_invalid:0,latent_valid:0,latent_sum:0,course_sum:0,noncourse_sum:0,components:Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(LATENT_AUDIT_WEIGHTS562).map(k=>[k,0])),strata:Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(LATENT_AUDIT_WEIGHTS562).map(k=>[k,{none:audit562Bucket(),partial:audit562Bucket(),all_six:audit562Bucket()}])),bins:Array.from({length:6},()=>Array.from({length:5},audit562Bucket)),top_bins:Array.from({length:5},audit562Bucket),top_ties:0};} function audit562Normalized(v){return typeof v==='number'&&Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0&&v<=1;} function audit562Latent(c){ if(c.model!==FOURTH_MODEL||c.version!=='v5-technique-evidence-provisional'||!sixBoats70(c.rows))return null; } const fmt=b=>({...b,hit_rate_percent:b.races?Math.round(b.hits/b.races*10000)/100:null,head_hit_rate_percent:b.races?Math.round(b.head_hits/b.races*10000)/100:null,roi_percent:b.stake_yen?Math.round(b.returned_yen/b.stake_yen*10000)/100:null,profit_yen:b.returned_yen-b.stake_yen}); } function outerTables560(html){ // Enumerate balanced tables, removing nested tables from their parent's direct rows. const source=String(html),re=/<\/?table\b[^>]*>/gi,stack=[],out=[];let m; while((m=re.exec(source))){ if(!/^<\//.test(m[0])){if(stack.length>=16)return [];stack.push({open:m.index,start:re.lastIndex,children:[]});continue;} if(!stack.length)continue; } function learn570Params(p){return !!p&&Number.isFinite(p.scale)&&p.scale>=.5&&p.scale<=2&&Array.isArray(p.bias)&&p.bias.length===6&&p.bias.every(x=>Number.isFinite(x)&&Math.abs(x)<=1.000001);} function learn570Fit(samples){ // Deterministic L2-regularized multinomial log calibration: shared slope + six boat intercepts. const a={scale:1,bias:Array(6).fill(0)},n=samples.length;if(!n)return null;
